[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>

For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. \"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\n\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time.
